Z:\claude-sota-installed\CLAUDE.md:155: ### CR-12 disposition lattice (5 classes — Wave 134 Fire 27 series codification)
Z:\claude-sota-installed\CLAUDE.md:157: Beyond the **install-source priority order** (PRIMARY/SECONDARY/TERTIARY) above, every upstream-vs-incumbent relationship falls into ONE of 5 classes. Wave 134 Fire 27 series empirically mapped all 5:
Z:\claude-sota-installed\CLAUDE.md:159: 1. **GENUINELY-NEW** — no Anthropic/incumbent parallel exists for the primitive's scope. Disposition: **INSTALL via CR-12 PRIMARY path** (official-native-channel per CR-6). No further audit needed beyond Probe DAG.
Z:\claude-sota-installed\CLAUDE.md:161: 2. **DUPLICATE-FUNCTIONALITY** — full parallel exists in Anthropic/eee stack covering same scope and mechanism. Disposition: **REJECT-FOR-FIT** per `Z:/claude-sota/.claude/rules/kiss-dry-yagni.md` Must-Never #4 (no duplicate functionality without clear reason).
Z:\claude-sota-installed\CLAUDE.md:163: 3. **PARTIAL-OVERLAP** — incumbent and candidate cover overlapping function via DIFFERENT MECHANISMS. Disposition: **CASE-BY-CASE**, typically CITE-PATTERN-ONLY or STUDY-PILOT-PATTERN-EXTRACT. Example: Wave 134 Fire 27-C mem0 (fact-extraction-based memory) vs eee graphiti (temporal-KG-based) vs mcp-memory (sqlite-vec-based) — same memory layer, different mechanisms. Z:\claude-sota-installed\CLAUDE.md:165: 4. **PROVIDER-COMPLEMENT** — parallel API surfaces but different scopes; both can coexist. Disposition: **INSTALL as ALTERNATIVE (not PRIMARY)** — primary incumbent retains canonical position. Example: Wave 134 Fire 27-A openai-agents-python (vs Anthropic `claude-agent-sdk-python` @ HEAD `b512f256`) — Anthropic SDK = direct CC control plane (PRIMARY); OpenAI SDK = provider-agnostic orchestration (ALTERNATIVE). Z:\claude-sota-installed\CLAUDE.md:167: 5. **ECOSYSTEM-IMPORT** — core primitive GENUINELY-NEW at the surface level but imports parallel ecosystem at the dependency level. Disposition: **EVALUATE ecosystem cost vs core primitive value** (often CITE-PATTERN-ONLY when ecosystem footprint disproportionate). Example: Wave 134 Fire 27-B langgraph (Pregel BSP graph execution is novel BUT requires `langchain-core>=1.4.0a2` + 6+ langgraph-* sub-package import + LangSmith proprietary recommended). Z:\claude-sota-installed\docs\sota-installed-manifest.md:264: ### `@latest` resolution at execute time (per Wave 50 fire 8 Pattern A on Agent I F-4 — operationalizes CR-9 version-pin mandate)
Z:\claude-sota-installed\docs\sota-installed-manifest.md:266: Every install command in this manifest tagged `@latest` (e.g., `npm install -g @doobidoo/mcp-memory-service@latest`, `docker pull qdrant/qdrant:latest`, `npm install -g markdownlint-cli2@latest`) carries **implicit `@latest-acknowledged-D6-risk:2026-05-06` status** — the manifest planning intent is "pull newest at install execution per CR-6, with explicit D6 today-release-auto-upgrade risk acknowledgment per CR-9". At install execution, the operator MUST:
Z:\claude-sota-installed\docs\sota-installed-manifest.md:268: 1. Resolve the actual version (e.g., `npm view @doobidoo/mcp-memory-service version` returns `1.4.7` → install command becomes `npm install -g @doobidoo/mcp-memory-service@1.4.7`)
Z:\claude-sota-installed\docs\sota-installed-manifest.md:270: 3. Pin the manifest install command to the resolved version post-install (manifest row updated from `@latest` to `@<resolved-version>` as part of `STAGED → INSTALLED` promotion)
Z:\claude-sota-installed\docs\sota-installed-manifest.md:273: **Rationale**: the manifest's `@latest` install commands ARE the SOTA-discipline contract per CR-6 fresh-from-github (do not pre-pin to stale versions in the planning document). The resolution-at-execute-time + provenance-recording gate satisfies CR-9 version-pin mandate without violating CR-6 fresh-from-github. The promotion-gate state machine above enforces this — a row cannot advance `STAGED → INSTALLED` until step 3 (resolved-version pin) lands in the manifest row.
Z:\claude-sota-installed\docs\sota-installed-manifest.md:275: **Scope**: applies to ALL install commands carrying raw `@latest` AND `@HEAD`-equivalent pulls (`git clone --depth 1 ... origin/main` without SHA pin). Does NOT apply to `@<version-tag>` pinned install commands (those satisfy CR-9 directly).
